WinPalm licenses and third-party notices

Generated from exact locked sources for Windows x64 and ARM64, including native runtime notices, nested upstream texts, and Rust build dependencies. Web production dependencies are conservatively included, with Tailwind CSS and Vite helpers. The presence of a notice does not imply every component is shipped. Line endings and trailing horizontal whitespace are normalized; upstream wording and provenance are retained.

WinPalm
Copyright (c) RS Application
Licensed under GNU Affero General Public License version 3 only.

WinPalm adapts CmdPalm's Rust host engine and Vue phone client for Windows.
CmdPalm source: https://github.com/rahatsagor/CmdPalm
Source baseline: version 0.7.0, commit 1d95d08451a4d88f7732cac5e3035d3bbe4840e7
Subsequent shared Deck, device backup and dictation improvements were adapted
from CmdPalm 0.8.0, commit f2d757c21da57df18c4d271987f868c83e44a794.
Further screen mirroring, pairing recovery, TLS and input ownership fixes
were adapted from CmdPalm through commit 4d53451 on 17 November 2026.
CmdPalm's original AGPL-3.0-only license is preserved in LICENSE.

The Windows desktop host, Windows input backend, packaging and Windows
adaptations are part of WinPalm. Third-party dependencies retain their
respective licenses. See THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md and the runtime notices
included with binary distributions.

When distributing WinPalm binaries, make the corresponding source available
under the AGPL and comply with the licenses of bundled dependencies.
